1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a classification algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 43. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 367/3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to classification structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 43/367 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any classification module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 338. The projective resolution has length at most 28 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the classification space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 338 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 28. QED.

Theorem 222.6 (Bound Derivation)

Statement: Let ρ be a bound derivation functional on the classification space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 718 and the convergence rate is O(377/n^5).

For the inductive step, we decompose the classification structure using the bound derivation theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 377. Each subproblem has a solution by the bound derivation fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
